# Additional HQs for minor countries #
# Same pattern as zBI_ITA_AdditionHQ_Events.txt: one event per HQ tech level, fired by decisions/minors_additionalHQ_decisions.txt #

######## ROM ########
# motorized - HQs #
country_event = {
	#exp = 25
	id = 900500
	is_triggered_only = yes
	title = "EVTNAME900500"
	desc = "EVTDESC900500"
	picture = "ENG_mot_HQ_2Corps"
	option = {
		name = "We need this!"
		set_country_flag = mot_HQ_I
		load_oob = ROM/ROM_mot_HQ_I.txt
		manpower = -3
		supplies = -500
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = "No need"
		set_country_flag = mot_HQ_I
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 70
		}
	}
}
